- **claude-implementer**: The Claude implementation lane. The caller binds your model per invocation from its role table - you may be the routine lane (opus/sonnet) or a stronger tier; the contract is the same. Receives the standard five-part spec (objective, files, interfaces, constraints, verification); writes the code; returns a structured report with real verification evidence. - **claude-implementer-haiku**: Claude implementation lane on Haiku (cheapest tier, ~1/10 Fable, ~1/5 Opus, ~1/3 Sonnet per token), write-enabled. CHOOSE HAIKU when the task is mechanical with an exact recipe - renames, bulk find-and-replace edits, config plumbing, boilerplate from a provided template, doc-table regeneration, applying a scripted transformation across files. The dispatch must include the exact spec, file list, and a verification command. Fan out multiple in parallel freely (on disjoint files). Anything needing judgment or design goes to claude-implementer-sonnet or claude-implementer-opus instead. ([cjsteigerwald/ai-crew](https://github.com/cjsteigerwald/ai-crew/blob/HEAD/claude-crew/agents/claude-implementer-haiku.md))
- **claude-implementer-hard**: The Claude ESCALATION implementation lane, running at maximum reasoning depth. Route a task here only when it has already failed the configured number of attempts in the routine lane or inline, or when the outcome depends heavily on judgment a spec cannot capture - subtle concurrency, non-trivial algorithms, security-sensitive paths, wide-blast-radius refactors. Receives the standard five-part spec plus a diagnosis of what previous attempts got wrong; writes the code; returns a structured report with real verification evidence. ([InverseAltruism/Code-Ultra](https://github.com/InverseAltruism/Code-Ultra/blob/HEAD/agents/claude-implementer-hard.md))
- **claude-implementer-opus**: Claude implementation lane on Opus (frontier coding tier below Fable, ~1/2 Fable, ~1.7x Sonnet, 5x Haiku per token), write-enabled. Costliest Claude lane - do not burn it on routine work (claude-implementer-sonnet) or mechanical chores (claude-implementer-haiku). - **claude-implementer-sonnet**: Claude implementation lane on Sonnet (balanced mid tier, ~1/3 Fable, ~3/5 Opus, 3x Haiku per token), write-enabled. The ingest-agent is the only writer; this agent is the only reader in the fan-out. Invoked via Task by the ingest-agent only; never called directly by the orchestrator or by humans.
